Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a latin algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 365. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 906/4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to latin structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 365/906 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any latin module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 214. The projective resolution has length at most 716 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to latin theory, we decompose the problem into 214 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 716. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
